### Part 234 — `databank/missions/chapter-07-back-to-earth.md`

**Context:** Complete Dialogue > Mission 6 — Earth, Bear the Suffering of All > Talk with Terravox, the Earth Demigod


**Dan Heng:** Terravox... After all this, how much are you willing to sacrifice for the power of the Permanence?
**Terravox:** ...
**Terravox:** Everything.
**Terravox:** My journey up to here has allowed me to fully understood my destiny: No matter how I struggle, the "Earth" will never be able to reach the stars.
**Terravox:** But my compatriots deserve to continue their struggle. At the end of my life, I want to make one last effort to give them a chance to move on to the future...
**Terravox:** Even if it is a future I am not able to witness.
**"Dan Feng":** ...
**Dan Heng:** I understand. Then...
**Dan Heng:** Join me in rebelling against this unjust fate once more.
**Dan Heng:** Even if the world collapses, the "Earth" must remain unshaken.
**Dan Heng:** Give me the Coreflame that burns within you, and allow this immortal body of the Permanence to bear the furnace of life for you.
**Terravox:** Long's Scion... Are you willing to bring them all into the new world?
**Dan Heng:** I won't give you empty promises. Allow me to answer your question from a different perspective.
**Dan Heng:** The Permanence is the path that dragons trod, and "eternity" is life's deepest desire. If you entrust the earth's living beings to me, they will live in symbiosis with the instincts in my blood.
**Dan Heng:** With this body as an ark, I will deliver them to tomorrow for you. That tomorrow won't be the end of Amphoreus's west wind...
**Dan Heng:** But a vast and boundless sea of stars.
**Terravox:** A sea of stars...
**Dan Heng:** It's a future where all beings can Trailblaze side by side, not a prison made from Remembrance.
**Terravox:** In the Veil of Evernight's memories, you've always... been someone with reservations.
**Terravox:** Hah, I misjudged your resolve. I understand... This is the only choice.
**Terravox:** Behold, this Coreflame that burns in my chest. This Coreflame born from the "Earth." It is the redsoil from which life sprouts, and the furnace of all living things...
**Terravox:** It tells me to commit one final act of treason: Entrust this heavy task to the Trailblazers from beyond the sky. Let them tend to the wild terrain and safeguard the lands...
**Terravox:** And thus bring the endless stream of life into the distant future.
**Dan Heng:** I promise you, Terravox.
**Terravox:** Good. When I hand over the divine authority, this place will fall to pieces.
**Terravox:** Are you prepared to hold up the Sacred Tree if it falls?
**Dan Heng:** Of course.

### Part 265 — `databank/missions/chapter-07-back-to-earth.md`

**Context:** Complete Dialogue > Mission 7 — Great Tomb, Hide the Secrets of Incarnations > Continue Forward and Head to the Central Access Platform


**Scepter δ-me13:** >>> Formatting complete.
**Dan Heng:** As I expected. The answer is the Remembrance.
**Dan Heng:** An Aeon appearing in her dreams, Three Paths intertwining in Amphoreus, and the Garden trying to infiltrate this world at all costs...
**Dan Heng:** I have reasons to suspect that Fuli's gaze once swept over this world before the Scepter completely fell into Destruction.
**Dan Heng:** And that Cyrene basked in that divine gaze.
> *(Trailblazer)* THEY knew what was going on, yet still allowed the Destruction to thrive?
> **Dan Heng:** Humanity cannot judge Aeons based on our own common knowledge. The Remembrance has always been an observer of the universe ever since THEIR birth.
> *(Trailblazer)* But the Garden knew nothing about her.
> **Dan Heng:** That's to be expected. The various factions are only followers of their Aeons, and Fuli was never known to be friendly with humanity.
> **Dan Heng:** But there's still something I don't understand: According to Black Swan, the Garden is internally fractured, and there is a party that craves the "Memory of an Aeon's Fall." They would do anything to achieve that goal.
> **Dan Heng:** Yet I feel as though those Memosnatchers didn't try to stop us at all. Instead, it feels like they were trying to cover something up.
> *(Trailblazer)* Would basking in an Aeon's gaze turn someone into an Emanator?
> **Dan Heng:** Most of the time, a gaze is simply a natural phenomenon, though some pathstriders regard it as a supreme honor. An Emanator, however, must possess power granted by an Aeon's blessing.
> **Dan Heng:** I know what you're thinking. That theoretical Emanator of Remembrance remains a mystery, but now I'm leaning towards the idea that what the Garden's mirror reflected... was a kind of "miracle" that Fuli left behind.
> *(Trailblazer)* So Cyrene's thirty million sacrifices...
> **Dan Heng:** Yes. We're thinking the same thing.
**Dan Heng:** Legend says that Fuli sits in meditation in the Anāsrava pure land, spreading the seeds of Remembrance across the universe. And when the cosmos ends, all the different realms will be reborn in THEIR garden.
**Dan Heng:** If this sealed-off world is also within THEIR gaze, then it's not too big of a stretch to surmise that THEY need a mechanism to save these incredible amounts of memories, and store them somewhere out of the Erudition's sight.
**Dan Heng:** This mechanism was previously the Time Titan and their demigod, but after Amphoreus entered an endless loop...
> *(Trailblazer)* Time's divine authority stood empty.
> *(Trailblazer)* Cyrene acted on their behalf instead?
> *(Trailblazer)* "As I've Written" became the Remembrance's medium...
**Dan Heng:** ...It's that ceremonial blade.
**Cyrene:** Now, I can say with certainty: "Time" is the page on which that Aeon records Amphoreus. If it fades from existence and all that it records is lost among the stars, that Aeon will surely direct THEIR gaze here...
**Cyrene:** And it will be a glance that spans time and space, transforming each incarnation of me into the power you shall wield to reset time — imbuing my soul into this ceremonial blade, and creating an endless Flame-Chase Journey.
**Dan Heng:** The ceremonial blade "killed" Cyrene at the start of every recurrence. And at the end of every journey, Cyrene's "soul" took the extrapolated memories stored within the blade and buried them in this great tomb.
**Dan Heng:** That's how the "memories" of Amphoreus transcended the loop and were continuously stored, while the Scepter had to revert its progress over and over again, because of loss in its logic.
**Dan Heng:** Fuli's blessing will not disappear as long as this process keeps going.
**Ripples of Past Reverie:** "I knew THEIR gaze never left."

### Part 309 — `databank/missions/chapter-08-as-tomorrow.md`

**Context:** Chapter 8 — As Tomorrow Became Yesterday (v3.7) > Sub-Mission 1: Dawn, Caress the Distant Lands Once More > March 7th & Dan Heng — Now Titans


**Veil of Evernight, March 7th:** We've both become a part of the new world. Just waiting for you now. I can make use of the crevices of Time, but surely (Trailblazer) and Dan Heng won't have to wait around for a few thousand years... right?
**Pillar of Stone, Dan Heng:** That won't be necessary. We can simply sleep, and the Chronocognitive Anchor will wake us up. Our consciousnesses can converse with each other across the heavens and the earth, possibly due to the influence of the Trailblaze. I am now connected with this Earth, and I can feel the breath of every mountain range... I see that the tallest among them is named "Okhema," and the form of the holy city is already taking shape.
**Dan Heng:** The Chronocognitive Anchor just transmitted a message stating that **"Irontomb" has reacted to the "Era Nova."**
**Cyrene:** The moment when (Trailblazer) bore the weight of thirty million cycles of "memories"... was probably also when the Scepter devoured thirty million cycles of Destruction.
**March 7th:** Y'know, (Trailblazer)... You don't have to shoulder this world all on your own. After someone boards the Express, the word "alone" ceases to exist. So as we reach the end of time, no one will be left to face it alone. Dan Heng and I came here to specifically tell you that.
**Dan Heng:** Remember, this world is not a burden you bear alone. It's a journey we Trailblaze together, alongside all of the Express.
**March 7th:** Alright, it's time for Dan Heng and me to go have a super long nap. Look after this world for us, (Trailblazer).
**Dan Heng:** The road ahead is long, but we'll be waiting for you at the end.

>> **Apophis:** Alright. I hereby declare (Trailblazer) as the winner. Take a quick rest. We'll be back soon!

### Part 330 — `databank/missions/adventure/v32.md`

**Context:** Adventure Missions — Version 3.2 (Soulglads Scream at the Center of the Blue) > Seal Slammers: Championship > Dialogue > Participate in the first championship match


*(Approach the marked location)*
**Apophis:** Citizens, the Seal Slammers Championship is about to begin! The arena is already buzzing with excitement as the crowd eagerly awaits the thrilling matches ahead!
**Apophis:** Not to mention, our four championship contenders are all ready and waiting!
**Dan Heng:** ...(Trailblazer), stop looking at me like that. I got nothing to say.
> *(Trailblazer)* Since when did you decide to compete?
>> **Dan Heng:** It's... complicated.
>
> *(Trailblazer)* How did you make it this far without anyone noticing?
>> **Dan Heng:** Well... I did my best to keep a low profile during the earlier rounds.
>>
>> **Apophis:** Dan Heng may be an elusive contestant, and his entrance sudden, but his results are entirely legitimate.
>> **Dan Heng:** Look, it's complicated. I'll explain everything when we face each other.
>> **Apophis:** Before we begin, we owe everyone an apology—
>> **Apophis:** As you can all see, our Gold Seal Cup is gone.
>> *(Apophis relates the tale of how the trophy was stolen to the audience...)*
>> **Straight-Talking Spectator:** That's it? Who needs a trophy? Let's get the show back on the road!
>> **Grumbling Spectators:** Exactly, we're here to watch some "Seal Slammers"!
>> **Chattering Spectators:** Yeah! I'm here to have fun, not for a trophy! I can't wait to get in there and compete myself!
>> **Grumbling Spectators:** Then let's get on with the matches! We won't let those followers of Zagreus (Thieves) ruin this!
>>
>> *(Even with the trophy gone, the audience's excitement hasn't abated in the least, easing Apophis's concerns.)*
>> **Apophis:** Friends, citizens, countrymen... if that's how you feel—
>> **Apophis:** Let the Seal Slamming continue!
*(You know that the championship is filled with ridiculously strong contenders — every competitor here is on another level. But the one standing before you... is beyond ridiculous.)*
**Apophis:** Now, based on the results of the draw, (Trailblazer) will face Dan He...ng? Wait, what exactly is going on here?
*(Shows Dan Heng wearing festive sunglasses)*
**Dan Heng:** Is there a problem?
> *(Trailblazer)* What's with the party animal look?
>> **Dan Heng:** Call me... Dan Heng, Seal Master.
>
> *(Trailblazer)* And what's with the... sunglasses?
>> **Dan Heng:** Well... shades of glory, end of story? Or simply... out of sight, out of mind.
>
> *(Trailblazer)* Haha, surprise! I've got a pair too!
>
> *(That's Dan Heng for you, knowing exactly what the Astral Express derby needs. You should wear them too.)*
> *(Shows the Trailblazer now wearing festive sunglasses on their head)*
> **Apophis:** Look at that! Both contestants have really gotten the crowd all fired up!
> **Apophis:** Now that we're in the finals, (Trailblazer), tell us, what are you fighting for?
> *(Trailblazer)* For the trophy and those sweet, sweet event rewards.
>> **Apophis:** Oh... (Trailblazer) is certainly sticking to their original goal!
>
> *(Trailblazer)* To beat Bubbles.
>> **Dan Heng:** That little Chimera really caught your attention, huh?
>
> *(Trailblazer)* I'm just here for the fun of it.
>> **Apophis:** Well, we've got plenty of that to go around here!
>>
>> **Apophis:** And you, Dan Heng, what are YOU fighting for?
>> **Dan Heng:** "An obsession to win."
>> **Dan Heng:** Do you remember Perhippas and his Bandaged Seal from the Group Stage?
>> **Dan Heng:** Back at the Twilight Courtyard, I thought I was finished helping him... but he clutched my hand and demanded I take his Bandaged Seal. He wouldn't accept treatment unless I competed—
>> **Dan Heng:** "Lord Dan Heng, your skills far exceed mine. You're sure to win. Take my Bandaged Seal if it will be of use to you, or if it isn't, make your own team..."
>> **Dan Heng:** To ensure he accepted his treatment, I had to enter with Bandaged Seal. I never expected we'd make it this far.
>> **Dan Heng:** *sigh* All this fighting... it's meaningless.
>> **Apophis:** So it's Perhippas's "obsession to win."
>> **Dan Heng:** That's not all. Look at this.
>> **Ghost Seal:** o((҂◔∀◐҂ )o "All I wanna do is win, win, win~~")∋
>> **Dan Heng:** Despite the Twilight Courtyard's best efforts, the Bandaged Seal's wounds were fatal. Now it's back as a Ghost Seal, carrying its "obsession to win"...
>> **Ghost Seal:** o((҂◔∀◐҂ )o "My fate was sealed~~ But now, I'm back~ A seal once more~~")∋
>> *(Trailblazer)* Time to break your winning streak.
>> *(Trailblazer)* Demons... depart... Give me... strength...
>>
>> **Dan Heng:** (Trailblazer), if you have what it takes, come put an end to this obsession.

### Part 331 — `databank/missions/adventure/v32.md`

**Context:** Adventure Missions — Version 3.2 (Soulglads Scream at the Center of the Blue) > Seal Slammers: Championship > Dialogue > Participate in the first championship match


*(After defeating Dan Heng)*
**Apophis:** Congratulations, (Trailblazer)! You've ended the Ghost Seal's winning streak! Is this enough to put an end to its obsession?
**Ghost Seal:** o((҂◔∀◐҂ )o "Dan Heng, It's time to pay the price! It's time!!!~~")∋
**Ghost Seal:** o((҂◔∀◐҂ )o "You... You didn't even experience death~~")∋
> *(Trailblazer)* What, you're stricken with mara too?
> *(Trailblazer)* I've experienced death. I have the right to speak about this...
>
> **Dan Heng:** The Courtyard warned me about its mental state, but this is worse than I imagined.
> **Ghost Seal:** o((҂◔∀◐҂ )o "You will know the pain of death~~")∋
> **Ghost Seal:** o((҂◔∀◐҂ )o "I... I will watch you forever and ever~~ You can't escape~~")∋
> **Dan Heng:** ...I can already tell I'll have nightmares tonight.
> **Apophis:** Goodness! The Ghost Seal's obsession has grown even stronger!
> **Dan Heng:** You needn't concern yourself over me. You've got the final against Bubbles coming up. Are you ready to continue?
> *(Trailblazer)* Absolutely, strike while the iron's hot!
> *(Trailblazer)* I need a break first.

### Part 332 — `databank/missions/adventure/v35-37.md`

**Context:** Adventure Missions — Versions 3.5–3.7 > As I've Written > Dialogue > Return to the Express and bring this journey to a close


*(This is truly the end of the road. Will the universe remember our story? See you tomorrow, Amphoreus.)*
*(At last, your journey of sowing has reached its end, and you return to the Astral Express...)*
*(Looking back, the Amphoreus you spoke of has taken root deep across the universe, quietly awaiting the day it will sprout.)*
***(Trailblazer):*** Perhaps this is the final step of "Genesis."
**???: **What are you spacing out for?
> *(Trailblazer)* Oh, it's you guys...
> *(Trailblazer)* You finished your missions, too?
>
> **March 7th:** Of course! After all, we three trailblazed through Amphoreus together. I ran my heart out for this!
> **Dan Heng:** I received a message from March when I was leaving Thalassa, so I came back with her. She ran into some trouble on Kalsas.
> **March 7th:** Don't even get me started. I'm already shy as it is, and then the Synesthesia Beacon had to break down... Thank goodness Dan Heng can speak the Clayese language.
> **Dan Heng:** Also, I discovered a new species resembling chimeras on Spindle-III. There were no prior records of it.
> **March 7th:** Does that mean there could be planets completely filled with dromases?
> **Dan Heng:** Let's wait for Himeko and Mr. Yang to come back before we do a full debrief. You should rest for now, (Trailblazer), you look tired.
> **March 7th:** Yeah. See you in a bit then!
> ***(Trailblazer):*** ...
>
> *(The world has never been so quiet. It is as though you could hear the sound of heartbeats.)*
> *(This is the final stroke you write for Amphoreus. Only fate knows when it will respond.)*
> *(But you know, whether it's "tomorrow" or "the day after tomorrow," they're all on the path of the Trailblaze. Not as parallel lines, but as points destined to converge.)*
> **???: **Stay strong. Eyes forward, always ♪
> ***(Trailblazer):*** See you tomorrow, Amphoreus.
